📄 FILE: ./src/components/layout/Header.tsx
================================================================================
```
"use client";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Menu, ChevronDown, MessageCircle, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { site, allServices, serviceCategories } from "@/shared/config/site";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { cn } from "@/shared/lib/utils";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "motion/react";

export function Header() {
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  const [openMega, setOpenMega] = useState(false);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 10);
    onScroll();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);

  return (
    <header className={cn("sticky top-0 z-50 w-full transition-all duration-300 bg-[#0A192F]/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-white/5", scrolled ? "py-3 shadow-[0_10px_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)]" : "py-4")}>
      <div className="container-page flex items-center justify-between text-white">
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-xl">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-[#D4AF37] to-[#B8860B] text-black shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.4)]">PW</div>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline tracking-tight">PrimeWealth</span>
        </Link>
        
        <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
          <Link href="/" className="px-4 py-2 hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors">Home</Link>
          <Link href="/about" className="px-4 py-2 hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors">About</Link>
          <div className="relative" onMouseEnter={() => setOpenMega(true)} onMouseLeave={() => setOpenMega(false)}>
            <button className="flex items-center gap-1 px-4 py-2 hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors">
              Services <ChevronDown className={cn("h-4 w-4 transition-transform duration-300", openMega && "rotate-180")} />
            </button>
            <AnimatePresence>{openMega && <MegaMenu />}</AnimatePresence>
          </div>
          <Link href="/funding" className="px-4 py-2 hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors">Funding</Link>
          <Link href="/contact" className="px-4 py-2 hover:text-[#D4AF37] transition-colors">Contact</Link>
        </nav>

        <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-4">
          <Button asChild className="bg-[#25D366] text-white hover:bg-[#1eb857] rounded-full h-10 px-5 text-sm"><a href={site.whatsapp} target="_blank"><MessageCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4"/> WhatsApp</a></Button>
          <Button asChild className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#D4AF37] to-[#B8860B] text-black hover:opacity-90 rounded-full h-10 px-6 text-sm font-bold shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(212,175,55,0.3)]"><Link href="/contact">Get Started <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4"/></Link></Button>
        </div>
        <button className="lg:hidden p-2 text-white" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(true)}><Menu /></button>
      </div>
      <AnimatePresence>{mobileOpen && <MobileMenu onClose={() => setMobileOpen(false)} />}</AnimatePresence>
    </header>
  );
}

function MegaMenu() {
  // Balancing columns to avoid empty space
  const col1 = [
    { title: "AARAMBH", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "AARAMBH") },
    { title: "KAVACH", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "KAVACH") }
  ];
  const col2 = [
    { title: "PRAMANIT", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "PRAMANIT") }
  ];
  const col3 = [
    { title: "NIDHI", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "NIDHI") },
    { title: "VISTAR", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "VISTAR") }
  ];
  const col4 = [
    { title: "VIKAS", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "VIKAS") },
    { title: "PRABANDHIT", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "PRABANDHIT") },
    { title: "DIGITAL", items: allServices.filter(s => s.category === "DIGITAL") }
  ];

  return (
    <motion.div 
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15, scale: 0.98 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 15, scale: 0.98 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: "easeOut" }}
      className="absolute left-1/2 top-full w-[min(1200px,calc(100vw-2rem))] -translate-x-1/2 pt-4"
    >
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-[#D4AF37]/20 bg-[#0A192F]/95 backdrop-blur-2xl p-8 shadow-[0_30px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Top Glowing Line */}
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-[1px] bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-[#D4AF37]/60 to-transparent" />
        
        {/* Background subtle glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-40 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 blur-[100px] rounded-full pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-8 relative z-10">
          {[col1, col2, col3, col4].map((column, idx) => (
            <motion.div 
              key={idx}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.1 + idx * 0.05 }}
              className="space-y-8"
            >
              {column.map((group) => (
                <div key={group.title}>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#D4AF37] mb-3 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]" /> {group.title}
                  </h4>
                  <ul className="space-y-2">
                    {group.items.map((item) => (
                      <li key={item.slug}>
                        <Link 
                          href={`/services/${item.slug}`} 
                          className="text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex items-center group"
                        >
                          <span className="w-0 overflow-hidden group-hover:w-4 transition-all duration-300 flex items-center text-[#D4AF37]">
                            <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                          </span>
                          {item.name}
                        </Link>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              ))}
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Bottom CTA Area inside Mega Menu */}
        <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between relative z-10">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Need help choosing the right service for your business?</p>
          <Button asChild className="bg-[#D4AF37]/10 text-[#D4AF37] hover:bg-[#D4AF37] hover:text-black border border-[#D4AF37]/30 rounded-full h-10 px-6 transition-all">
            <Link href="/contact">Talk to an Expert</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function MobileMenu({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <motion.div initial={{ x: "100%" }} animate={{ x: 0 }} exit={{ x: "100%" }} className="fixed inset-y-0 right-0 w-[85%] max-w-sm bg-[#0A192F] shadow-2xl p-6 z-50 border-l border-white/10 overflow-y-auto">
      <button onClick={onClose} className="mb-8 font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2"><X className="h-5 w-5" /> Close</button>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-white">
        {["Home", "About", "Services", "Funding", "Contact"].map(i => (
          <Link key={i} href={i==="Home"?"/":`/${i.toLowerCase()}`} onClick={onClose} className="text-lg font-medium hover:text-[#D4AF37]">{i}</Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

📄 FILE: ./src/components/motion/CountUp.tsx
================================================================================
```
"use client";
import { useInView, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

export function CountUp({ to, suffix = "", duration = 1.5 }: { to: number; suffix?: string; duration?: number }) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
  const inView = useInView(ref, { once: true });
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  const [n, setN] = useState(reduce ? to : 0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!inView || reduce) return;
    let raf = 0; const start = performance.now();
    const tick = (t: number) => {
      const p = Math.min((t - start) / (duration * 1000), 1);
      setN(Math.round(to * (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 3))));
      if (p < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [inView, to, duration, reduce]);

  return <span ref={ref}>{n}{suffix}</span>;
}
```

📄 FILE: ./src/components/motion/Reveal.tsx
================================================================================
```
"use client";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export function Reveal({ children, delay = 0, y = 20, className }: { children: ReactNode; delay?: number; y?: number; className?: string }) {
  const reduce = useReducedMotion();
  return (
    <motion.div className={className} initial={reduce ? false : { opacity: 0, y }} whileInView={reduce ? {} : { opacity: 1, y: 0 }} viewport={{ once: true, margin: "-100px" }} transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay, ease: [0.25, 1, 0.5, 1] }}>
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

"use client";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
export const staggerContainer = { hidden: {}, visible: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.1 } } };
export const staggerItem = { hidden: { opacity: 0, y: 20 }, visible: { opacity: 1, y: 0, transition: { duration: 0.5 } } };
export function StaggerContainer({ children, className }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return <motion.div variants={staggerContainer} initial="hidden" whileInView="visible" viewport={{ once: true, margin: "-100px" }} className={className}>{children}</motion.div>;
}

"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "motion/react";
import { MessageSquare, X, Send, Bot, User } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";

type Message = { role: "ai" | "user"; text: string; options?: string[] };

export function AIChatWidget() {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([
    { role: "ai", text: "Hi! I'm PrimeWealth AI. How can I help you today?", options: ["Funding/Loans", "Company Registration", "Certifications"] }
  ]);

  const handleOptionClick = (option: string) => {
    const newMessages = [...messages, { role: "user" as const, text: option }];
    setMessages(newMessages);

    // Simulate AI thinking and responding
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (option === "Funding/Loans") {
        setMessages([...newMessages, { role: "ai", text: "Great! We offer CGTMSE, PMEGP, and MUDRA loans. Do you have an MSME/Udyam certificate?", options: ["Yes", "No"] }]);
      } else if (option === "Company Registration") {
        setMessages([...newMessages, { role: "ai", text: "We can register your Pvt Ltd, LLP, or OPC in 7 days. Would you like a free consultation?", options: ["Book Consultation", "View Pricing"] }]);
      } else if (option === "Certifications") {
        setMessages([...newMessages, { role: "ai", text: "We help with Startup India, GST, ISO, and FSSAI. Which one do you need?", options: ["Startup India", "GST/FSSAI"] }]);
      } else if (option === "No") {
        setMessages([...newMessages, { role: "ai", text: "No problem. We can get your Udyam certificate first, which is free, and then apply for loans. Want to start?", options: ["Get Udyam Certificate", "Talk to Expert"] }]);
      } else {
        setMessages([...newMessages, { role: "ai", text: "Perfect. Our expert will contact you shortly. You can also WhatsApp us directly for faster response!", options: ["Close Chat"] }]);
      }
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex flex-col items-end">
      <AnimatePresence>
        {isOpen && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, scale: 0.95 }}
            className="bg-[#0B1120] border border-white/10 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-[350px] overflow-hidden mb-4 flex flex-col"
          >
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="bg-[#112240] p-4 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#D4AF37] to-[#B8860B] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Bot className="h-5 w-5 text-black" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">PrimeWealth AI</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-green-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-500 animate-pulse" /> Online
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white"><X className="h-5 w-5" /></button>
            </div>

            {/* Chat Area */}
            <div className="p-4 h-[300px] overflow-y-auto flex flex-col gap-4">
              {messages.map((msg, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={`flex ${msg.role === "user" ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"}`}>
                  <div className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${msg.role === "user" ? "bg-[#D4AF37] text-black rounded-tr-sm" : "bg-[#112240] text-slate-300 border border-white/5 rounded-tl-sm"}`}>
                    {msg.text}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {/* Options */}
              {messages[messages.length - 1].options && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2">
                  {messages[messages.length - 1].options?.map((opt) => (
                    <button
                      key={opt}
                      onClick={() => handleOptionClick(opt)}
                      className="text-xs bg-white/5 border border-white/10 hover:border-[#D4AF37] hover:text-[#D4AF37] text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors"
                    >
                      {opt}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Input Area (Fake) */}
            <div className="p-3 border-t border-white/5 bg-[#112240]">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#0A192F] rounded-full p-1 border border-white/10">
                <input type="text" placeholder="Type a message..." className="bg-transparent text-sm w-full outline-none px-3 text-white placeholder:text-slate-500" disabled />
                <button className="bg-[#D4AF37] p-2 rounded-full text-black"><Send className="h-4 w-4" /></button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Floating Button */}
      <motion.button
        whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
        whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        className="h-16 w-16 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#D4AF37] to-[#B8860B] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(212,175,55,0.4)] relative"
      >
        {isOpen ? <X className="h-7 w-7 text-black" /> : <MessageSquare className="h-7 w-7 text-black" />}
        {!isOpen && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-red-500 rounded-full border-2 border-[#0A192F] animate-pulse" />}
      </motion.button>
    </div>
  );
}

```
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { forwardRef } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/shared/lib/utils";

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md font-medium transition-all duration-200 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        primary: "bg-brand-600 text-white hover:bg-brand-700 shadow-soft hover:shadow-hover",
        secondary: "bg-surface-2 text-foreground border border-border hover:bg-surface-3",
        ghost: "text-foreground hover:bg-surface-2",
        link: "text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700 underline-offset-4 hover:underline px-0 h-auto",
        whatsapp: "bg-[#25D366] text-white hover:bg-[#1eb857] shadow-soft hover:shadow-hover",
      },
      size: { sm: "h-9 px-3 text-sm", md: "h-11 px-5 text-sm", lg: "h-12 px-6 text-base", icon: "h-10 w-10" },
    },
    defaultVariants: { variant: "primary", size: "md" },
  }
);

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> { asChild?: boolean; }

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button";
    return <Comp ref={ref} className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)} {...props} />;
  }
);
Button.displayName = "Button";
```

📄 FILE: ./src/components/ui/CustomCursor.tsx
================================================================================
```
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { motion, useMotionValue, useSpring } from "motion/react";

export function CustomCursor() {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
  const cursorX = useMotionValue(-100);
  const cursorY = useMotionValue(-100);
  const springConfig = { damping: 25, stiffness: 300, mass: 0.5 };
  const cursorXSpring = useSpring(cursorX, springConfig);
  const cursorYSpring = useSpring(cursorY, springConfig);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (window.matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)").matches) return;
    const moveCursor = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      cursorX.set(e.clientX - 16);
      cursorY.set(e.clientY - 16);
      if (!isVisible) setIsVisible(true);
    };
    const handleMouseLeave = () => setIsVisible(false);
    const handleMouseEnter = () => setIsVisible(true);
    window.addEventListener("mousemove", moveCursor);
    document.body.addEventListener("mouseleave", handleMouseLeave);
    document.body.addEventListener("mouseenter", handleMouseEnter);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("mousemove", moveCursor);
      document.body.removeEventListener("mouseleave", handleMouseLeave);
      document.body.removeEventListener("mouseenter", handleMouseEnter);
    };
  }, [cursorX, cursorY, isVisible]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  return (
    <motion.div
      className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-[#D4AF37] bg-[#D4AF37]/20 backdrop-blur-sm pointer-events-none z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center mix-blend-screen"
      style={{ x: cursorXSpring, y: cursorYSpring }}
    >
      <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-[#D4AF37] rounded-full" />
    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

📄 FILE: ./src/components/ui/EMICalculator.tsx

```
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "motion/react";
import { IndianRupee, Percent, Calendar, TrendingUp } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";

export function EMICalculator() {
  const [loanAmount, setLoanAmount] = useState(5000000); // 50 Lakhs
  const [interestRate, setInterestRate] = useState(10.5);
  const [tenure, setTenure] = useState(5); // Years

  // EMI Calculation Formula
  const monthlyRate = interestRate / 12 / 100;
  const months = tenure * 12;
  const emi = (loanAmount * monthlyRate * Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, months)) / (Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, months) - 1);
  const totalPayment = emi * months;
  const totalInterest = totalPayment - loanAmount;

  const formatCurrency = (val: number) => {
    if (val >= 10000000) return `₹${(val / 10000000).toFixed(2)} Cr`;
    if (val >= 100000) return `₹${(val / 100000).toFixed(2)} Lakh`;
    return `₹${val.toLocaleString('en-IN')}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#0F172A]/90 backdrop-blur-xl border border-[#D4AF37]/30 rounded-[2.5rem] p-8 md:p-12 shadow-[0_20px_80px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-[#D4AF37]/10 blur-[100px] rounded-full pointer-events-none" />
      
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-12 relative z-10">
        
        {/* Sliders Area */}
        <div className="space-y-10">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Calculate Your EMI</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Estimate your monthly loan payments for MSME schemes.</p>
          </div>

          {/* Loan Amount */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2"><IndianRupee className="h-4 w-4 text-[#D4AF37]"/> Loan Amount</label>
              <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#D4AF37]">{formatCurrency(loanAmount)}</span>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="range" min="100000" max="20000000" step="100000" value={loanAmount} 
              onChange={(e) => setLoanAmount(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-[#D4AF37]" 
            />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2"><span>₹1 Lakh</span><span>₹2 Cr</span></div>
          </div>

          {/* Interest Rate */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2"><Percent className="h-4 w-4 text-[#D4AF37]"/> Interest Rate (p.a.)</label>
              <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#D4AF37]">{interestRate}%</span>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="range" min="5" max="20" step="0.1" value={interestRate} 
              onChange={(e) => setInterestRate(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-[#D4AF37]" 
            />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2"><span>5%</span><span>20%</span></div>
          </div>

          {/* Tenure */}
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2"><Calendar className="h-4 w-4 text-[#D4AF37]"/> Tenure (Years)</label>
              <span className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#D4AF37]">{tenure} Years</span>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="range" min="1" max="15" step="1" value={tenure} 
              onChange={(e) => setTenure(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full h-2 bg-white/10 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-[#D4AF37]" 
            />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2"><span>1 Yr</span><span>15 Yrs</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Results Area */}
        <div className="bg-[#112240] rounded-3xl p-8 border border-white/5 flex flex-col justify-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Monthly EMI</p>
          <motion.p 
            key={emi}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            className="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-extrabold text-white mb-8"
          >
            ₹{isNaN(emi) ? "0" : Math.round(emi).toLocaleString('en-IN')}
          </motion.p>
          
          <div className="space-y-4 pt-6 border-t border-white/10">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Principal Amount</p>
              <p className="font-bold text-white">{formatCurrency(loanAmount)}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Total Interest</p>
              <p className="font-bold text-[#D4AF37]">{formatCurrency(totalInterest)}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Total Payable</p>
              <p className="font-bold text-green-400">{formatCurrency(totalPayment)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-8">
            <Button asChild className="w-full h-14 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#D4AF37] to-[#B8860B] text-black font-bold text-lg hover:opacity-90">
              <a href="#apply">Apply for this Loan</a>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

📄 FILE: ./src/components/ui/LiveNotifications.tsx
================================================================================
```
"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "motion/react";
import { CheckCircle2, X } from "lucide-react";

const notifications = [
  { name: "Rahul S.", city: "Mumbai", action: "got CGTMSE Loan Approved", amount: "₹50,00,000" },
  { name: "Priya P.", city: "Bangalore", action: "got DPIIT Certificate", amount: "Startup India" },
  { name: "Amit K.", city: "Delhi", action: "registered Private Limited", amount: "7 Days" },
  { name: "Neha G.", city: "Ahmedabad", action: "got PMEGP Subsidy", amount: "₹15,00,000" },
  { name: "Vikram R.", city: "Pune", action: "got GST Registration", amount: "Complete" },
];

export function LiveNotifications() {
  const [active, setActive] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let index = 0;
    const showRandomNotification = () => {
      setActive(notifications[index]);
      setIsVisible(true);
      setTimeout(() => setIsVisible(false), 5000); // Hide after 5s
      index = (index + 1) % notifications.length;
    };

    // Show first one after 3 seconds
    const initialTimeout = setTimeout(showRandomNotification, 3000);
    
    // Then repeat every 12 seconds
    const interval = setInterval(showRandomNotification, 12000);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(initialTimeout);
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-6 left-6 z-50">
      <AnimatePresence>
        {isVisible && active && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -50, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -50, y: 20 }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 300, damping: 25 }}
            className="bg-[#112240] border border-[#D4AF37]/30 p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-4 w-[320px] relative overflow-hidden group"
          >
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#D4AF37]/10 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
            <div className="h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-[#D4AF37]/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 text-[#D4AF37]" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-300">
                <span className="font-bold text-white">{active.name}</span> from {active.city} {active.action}.
              </p>
              <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#D4AF37] mt-1">{active.amount}</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => setIsVisible(false)} className="text-slate-500 hover:text-white">
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}
```

"use client";
export function Marquee() {
  const items = ["Startup India", "GeM Portal", "MSME Ministry", "Make In India", "ZED Scheme", "NSIC", "Udyam Portal", "DPIIT"];
  return (
    <div className="flex overflow-hidden w-full bg-[#050B14] border-y border-white/5 py-4">
      <div className="flex whitespace-nowrap animate-marquee items-center gap-12 px-6 opacity-50">
        {[...items, ...items].map((item, i) => (
          <span key={i} className="text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-widest uppercase">{item}</span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  id: string;
  name: string;
  provider: string;
  category: "grant" | "loan" | "vc" | "debt";
  amount: string;
  interest?: string;
  collateral?: string;
  tenure?: string;
  description: string;
  eligibility: string[];
  benefits: string[];
}

📄 FILE: ./src/shared/lib/utils.ts
================================================================================
```
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";
export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) { return twMerge(clsx(inputs)); }
```
